## Details

### Coddy — 🔴 update recommended

Template repo [RockinPaul/coddy_railway_template](https://github.com/RockinPaul/coddy_railway_template) (last commit 2026-10-15) · [Railway listing](https://railway.com/deploy/coddy)

> Thin wrapper that copies upstream's static Go binary out of its published scratch image onto Alpine (scratch has no shell for an entrypoint). The pin is the image tag = git tag <version>, no v prefix. MIT. See guide §5.27.

### DSH + LongMemory — 🔴 update recommended

Template repo [RockinPaul/dsh_railway_template](https://github.com/RockinPaul/dsh_railway_template) (last commit 2026-10-16) · [Railway listing](https://railway.com/deploy/dsh-longmemory)

> Upstream ships prereleases only (alpha/rc every few days); the pin is an npm version whose git tag is dsh-v<version>. LongMemory is pinned in its own template repo. Sign-in is a generated DSH_GATE_PASSWORD, not the launch token: DSH mints that token with randomBytes() per process and it cannot be preset, so the gateway redeems it behind the edge gate and the deployer only sees a browser password prompt. The gate and auto sign-in are interlocked — clearing the password disables both. The gate and the redirect must live in a Caddy `route`, not a `handle`, or `redir` sorts ahead of `basic_auth` and an anonymous GET / is answered 302 with the live token; test-gateway.sh pins that case, and its stand-in must answer the exchange with the same Location as the pinned DSH (0.1.7 sends ./ where 0.1.5 sent /; the Caddyfile loop guard matches both). See guide §5.22.

  > mise now includes a language server, `mise lsp`, which gives editors diagnostics, completion, hover, navigation and quick fixes for `mise.toml`. Doctor checks can depend on other checks, and `mise dot history purge` erases a tracked file from dotfiles history. This release also fixes many lockfile, prune, Windows and dotfiles issues.
  >

  > 
  > - **`mise lsp`, a language server for mise config files.** It runs over stdin/stdout, so any editor with an LSP client can use it (Neovim, Helix, Emacs, Zed, VS Code, ...). It uses the schema and formatter of the installed mise, so its answers match you …
- [v2026.10.17](https://github.com/jdx/mise/releases/tag/v2026.10.17) 2026-10-20
  > `bootstrap.user.login_shell` now accepts a shell name such as `fish` instead of a full path. `[doctor.checks]` can use templates, and vfox backend plugins get their own cache directory. - [v2026.10.15](https://github.com/jdx/mise/releases/tag/v2026.10.15) 2026-10-19
  > The default 24-hour `minimum_release_age` now applies to every backend. Many more backends report release dates, so the cooldown can actually hold new versions back, and mise warns when it installs a version it couldn't date. This release also adds a PowerShell installer for Windows, `read_json`/`read_yaml`/`read_toml` template functions, `dotnet@lts`/`dotnet@sts`, and new dotfiles options.

  > ## Changed
  >

- [v2026.10.14](https://github.com/jdx/mise/releases/tag/v2026.10.14) 2026-10-18
  > A global `config.override.toml` can now take precedence over project config, and `mise config explain` describes each key in a config file. Paranoid mode can be turned on per category, and in that mode dotenv files need their own content-bound trust. Installs now fail when a tool has an option its backend doesn't read, which affects existing configs (see Changed).
  > 
  > ## Added
  > 
  > - **Global override files.** `~/.config/mise/config.override.toml`, and `config.<env>.override.toml` for each `MISE_ENV`, rank above every project file, including `mise.local.toml` and `mise.<env>.toml`. This covers `[env] …
- [v2026.10.13](https://github.com/jdx/mise/releases/tag/v2026.10.13) 2026-10-17
  > mise now ignores `[settings]` in a project config until you trust it. This release also adds per-version-range backend options to the registry and template support for command wrappers and `[bootstrap.repos]`. It fixes several bugs in lockfiles, tasks and multi-version tool options.
  > 
  > ## Security
  > 
  > - **`[settings]` from untrusted project configs is ignored.** Before, a cloned repo's `mise.toml` could change settings as soon as mise found it, with no trust prompt. That included settings that redirect downloads or feed build commands, such as `node.mirror_url`, `python.pyenv_repo` and `ruby.precom …

Newer releases (2):
- [v1.5.1](https://github.com/EverMind-AI/EverOS/releases/tag/v1.5.1) 2026-10-22
  > **Hybrid and vector search keep up with 10 requests a second out of the box.**
  > Each of those searches embeds its query first, and the slots reserved for
  > those calls were the limit: with 4 of them and a remote embedding endpoint
  > answering in about 0.45 s, search topped out near 9 requests a second and a
  > steady 10 queued without bound. The default is now 16, which held the same
  > load at a p50 of 0.42 s and a p95 of 1.45 s.
  > 
  > ## Changed
  > 
  > - **`[embedding].query_max_concurrent` defaults to 16 instead of 4.** Every
  >   hybrid or vector search makes one query embedding call, and the budget
  >   reserved for …
- [v1.5.0](https://github.com/EverMind-AI/EverOS/releases/tag/v1.5.0) 2026-10-21
  > **`/add` no longer has to wait for extraction, and an index can no longer be
  > wrong without saying so.** A client can hand a batch to `/add` with
  > `async_mode` and get a `202` back in milliseconds, while a durable in-process
  > queue runs extraction in session order; the field, the response body and the
  > task-status paths match EverOS Cloud, so one client works against both. Vector
  > columns now follow `[embedding] dim` instead of a hardcoded 1024, startup
  > refuses an index the configured model cannot have produced instead of
  > accepting writes that are truncated or searches that quietly return nothing, …

  > - Added rate limiting that locks out a client address for one minute after 10 invalid API keys within a minute, returning HTTP 429 with a `Retry-After` header for all subsequent requests, including those with a valid key.
  > - Excluded requests with missing API keys from the failure count, since they are not treated as guesses.
  > - Bounded the table of tracked cli …
- [v1.4.545](https://github.com/danielmiessler/Fabric/releases/tag/v1.4.545) 2026-10-23

  > ### PR [#2287](https://github.com/danielmiessler/Fabric/pull/2287) by [pcbeingused333](https://github.com/pcbeingused333): fix(fsdb): make existing session and context folders and files private
  > 
  > - Fixed session and context storage permissions so that configuration now strips group and other permission bits from the sessions and contexts folders, as well as the files within them.
  > - Addressed a privacy issue in which histories saved by earlier versions with 0755 and 0644 modes remained readable by other users, since Save preserves the mode of an existing file.

  > We are excited to announce the release of Mirage 0.0.7 🎉🎉🎉
  > 
  > Mirage 0.0.7 is a one-dispatcher release. 0.0.6 decided what an agent may do; 0.0.7 sends every way in through the same place, so that decision, the cache and the search hold alike for a command, a CLI, a runtime and every server endpoint. Commands read and write through the dispatcher, and every backend runs the same generic commands. Underneath, the command kernels move into one Rust crate that both hosts run, through PyO3 in Python and WebAssembly in TypeScript: sed, grep, rg, sort, jq, printf, bc, diff, cut and tr among them.   > **Note: This is a beta version and NOT ready for use in production.**
  > 
  > ### Security
  > 
  > 1. Serve internal service messaging (WAMP) on a separate internal port and require a shared secret to connect
  > 2. Require a valid password along with the 2FA code when logging in
  > 3. Only allow password reset with tokens from the password reset flow
  > 4. Rotate refresh tokens on every use, and revoke the tokens of deleted accounts
  > 5. Require logging in again before resolving the keys of a password change that failed midway
  > 6. Block the CORS proxy from fetching internal addresses, and isolate it on its own network …

  > ### 新增
  > 
  > - 两台 Octop 可以连起来用：通过云端协同使用对方的专家，能聊天，也能改对方工作区里的文件
  > - 专家可以组队。主持人负责分派任务和收尾总结，成员的回复会回到聊天，也会同步到即时通讯
  > - 新连接器：企查查、OpenAlex、语雀、Obsidian、Agent Mail，以及钉钉命令行（设备码登录后可查通讯录、日程和文档）
  > - 可以用公司的 LDAP 账号登录。Telegram 机器人可以限定哪些用户能跟它说话
  > - 记忆可以手改「用户档案」和「强记忆」。和已有记忆打架时先请你确认，不会悄悄覆盖；也可以备份和恢复
  > - 技能和技能包可以导出成一个 ZIP，换一台机器再导入。技能包可以关掉别人的打包下载
  > - 定时任务可以只跑一次，跑完自动停用，仍留在列表里。聊天进行中可以再补一句，不用等这一轮结束
  > - 每个专家可以设定默认对话方式：问答、规划或动手。侧栏可以按自己的账号重新排列
  > - 语音支持腾讯云边说边出字。当前模型不可用时，可以按健康状态自动换一个
  > - 知识库旁边可以按文件夹浏览文档。新增业务流程、知识工作等专家模板。内置助手可以回答产品和用法问题
  > - 别的程序可以通过 Agent MCP 网关调用这个 Agent。ONNX 模型可以不连 Hugging Face 直接加载

  > The default 24-hour `minimum_release_age` now applies to every backend.   > A global `config.override.toml` can now take precedence over project config, and `mise config explain` describes each key in a config file. Paranoid mode can be turned on per category, and in that mode dotenv files need their own content-bound trust. Installs now fail when a tool has an option its backend doesn't read, which affects existing configs (see Changed).
  >

- [v2026.10.13](https://github.com/jdx/mise/releases/tag/v2026.10.13) 2026-10-17
  > mise now ignores `[settings]` in a project config until you trust it. This release also adds per-version-range backend options to the registry and template support for command wrappers and `[bootstrap.repos]`. It fixes several bugs in lockfiles, tasks and multi-version tool options.
  > 
  > ## Security
  > 
  > - **`[settings]` from untrusted project configs is ignored.** Before, a cloned repo's `mise.toml` could change settings as soon as mise found it, with no trust prompt. ### QwenPaw — 🔴 update recommended

Template repo [RockinPaul/qwenpaw_railway_template](https://github.com/RockinPaul/qwenpaw_railway_template) (last commit 2026-09-16) · [Railway listing](https://railway.com/deploy/qwenpaw-1)

> Thin wrapper over upstream's own multi-arch image, used unmodified - only env vars and an entrypoint. Requires a login upstream leaves optional, and deliberately runs NO relay, because QwenPaw skips auth for 127.0.0.1/::1 and an in-container proxy would silently disable the login (measured 200 via relay vs 401 direct). Rebinding to :: was tried and reverted, since Python sets IPV6_V6ONLY and Railway's public proxy is IPv4. Auto-registration variables are write-once, and so is the agent language: upstream defaults to zh with no env var and no init flag, and its non-interactive init copies persona files with skip_existing=True, so the wrapper seeds config.json and runs init itself before handing off (QWENPAW_AGENT_LANGUAGE, default en). Apache-2.0. See guide §5.31.

### ReMe — 🔴 update recommended

Template repo [RockinPaul/reme_railway_template](https://github.com/RockinPaul/reme_railway_template) (last commit 2026-09-29) · [Railway listing](https://railway.com/deploy/reme)

> First template with NO upstream image (ReMe ships only a pip package) and NO upstream authentication - upstream states the service layer has none, CORS allows any origin, jobs may write/move/delete files, and instructs users to place it behind an authenticated reverse proxy rather than exposing it directly. So Caddy holds a generated bcrypt credential as the only public listener while ReMe keeps its default 127.0.0.1 bind and service.host=0.0.0.0 is never set. All 149 resolved packages are hash-pinned in requirements.lock because without an upstream image a rebuild is a fresh resolution; regenerate on linux/amd64. reme-ai declares reme_studio with no version constraint, so the lock is what stops the UI drifting. Apache-2.0. See guide §5.32.

### Scrumboy — 🔴 update recommended

Template repo [RockinPaul/scrumboy_railway_template](https://github.com/RockinPaul/scrumboy_railway_template) (last commit 2026-09-16) · [Railway listing](https://railway.com/deploy/scrumboy-1)

> Thin wrapper over upstream's own multi-arch image plus curl and tini. The entrypoint claims the Owner account against a loopback-only listener before the public port opens, because Scrumboy gives ownership to the first caller of /api/auth/bootstrap and serves anonymous reads and writes until then. Image tags are semver WITHOUT a v prefix; the git tag has one. AGPL-3.0. See guide \u00a75.29.

  > A global `config.override.toml` can now take precedence over project config, and `mise config explain` describes each key in a config file.   > mise now ignores `[settings]` in a project config until you trust it. This release also adds per-version-range backend options to the registry and template support for command wrappers and `[bootstrap.repos]`. It fixes several bugs in lockfiles, tasks and multi-version tool options.
  > 
  > ## Security
  > 
  > - **`[settings]` from untrusted project configs is ignored.** Before, a cloned repo's `mise.toml` could change settings as soon as mise found it, with no trust prompt. ### Trivy Server — 🔴 update recommended

Template repo [RockinPaul/trivy_railway_template](https://github.com/RockinPaul/trivy_railway_template) (last commit 2026-09-16) · [Railway listing](https://railway.com/deploy/trivy-server)

> Thin wrapper over upstream's own multi-arch image, used unmodified. Adds Railway environment plus a guard that refuses to start without TRIVY_TOKEN, because an empty token disables authentication entirely and upstream's own guidance for an exposed server is to configure one. No browser surface; the listing leads with the client command. Image and git tags are both plain semver with a v prefix on the git side. Apache-2.0. See guide \u00a75.30.

  > ## 🔐 Security update: eMudhra CA incident 🛡️

  > Trivy v0.76.1 includes mitigations for the [eMudhra intermediate CA key compromise](https://blog.cloudflare.com/emudhra-certificate-compromise/).

CHANGELOG lines added since the pin:
> ### Fixes
> - Embedded project-scoped writes. `longmemory_ingest` with a `project_id`,
>   `longmemory_remember_decision` and connector imports go through
>   `applyImportPlan`, which stored every node without a semantic vector, so
>   those memories never received a vector score in recall (#216).
> - The HTTP server never read `LONGMEMORY_TENANT_ID`/`LONGMEMORY_USER_ID`, so
>   every restart silently re-scoped the engine (and the `/mcp` endpoint) to the
>   `default` tenant and user, making memories saved under a configured scope
>   unreachable after a restart (#215).
> - MCP tool responses (`longmemory_ingest`, `longmemory_recall`, and others)
>   embedded each node's full embedding vector, repeated across every place the
>   node is nested in a response, inflating responses by thousands of tokens.
>   Vectors are now stripped by default (`LONGMEMORY_MCP_INCLUDE_VECTORS=true`
>   or a per-call `include_vectors: true` input restores them); ingest and
>   recall responses also no longer repeat a node already returned in full
>   elsewhere in the same response, and `structuredContent` — an unvalidated
>   duplicate of `content[].text`, since no tool declares an `outputSchema` — is
>   no longer emitted at all (#219 …

### PenguinHarness — 🟠 review

Template repo [RockinPaul/penguin_railway_template](https://github.com/RockinPaul/penguin_railway_template) (last commit 2026-10-16) · [Railway listing](https://railway.com/deploy/penguinharness)

> Thin wrapper over upstream's own multi-arch image, kept unmodified — its entrypoint already prepares the data root and drops to uid 1000 with exec setpriv. The wrapper adds Railway environment plus a guard that refuses to start without a pinned admin password. The pin is the image tag = git tag with a v prefix. Apache-2.0. See guide §5.28.

### Marmot — 🟡 minor drift

Template repo [RockinPaul/marmot_railway_template](https://github.com/RockinPaul/marmot_railway_template) (last commit 2026-10-16) · [Railway listing](https://railway.com/deploy/marmot)

> Thin wrapper over upstream's own multi-arch image plus su-exec. The entrypoint replaces Marmot's built-in admin:admin against a loopback-only listener before the public port opens, because upstream ships that credential and only tells you to change it after first login — on a public domain that would leave the catalog, the REST API and the MCP endpoint open to whoever tries the obvious pair. Image tags are semver WITHOUT a v prefix; the git tag has one, and upstream also publishes -previewN tags that the tag_pattern deliberately excludes. MARMOT_SERVER_ENCRYPTION_KEY must base64-decode to exactly 32 bytes, which ${{secret(N)}} cannot produce, so the key is derived as base64(sha256(MARMOT_ENCRYPTION_SEED)). Plugins (71 of them in 0.11.0, ~1.5 GB, no way to narrow the list) install from ghcr.io at every startup, so MARMOT_PLUGINS_DIR points at the 5120 MB volume to pay for it once; a bump can outgrow the volume while /readyz stays green, and the entrypoint clears the cache when the marmot version changes. MIT. See guide §5.34.

### PaddleOCR — 🟡 minor drift

Template repo [RockinPaul/paddleocr_railway_template](https://github.com/RockinPaul/paddleocr_railway_template) (last commit 2026-09-16) · [Railway listing](https://railway.com/deploy/paddleocr)

> Second template with no upstream image (PaddleOCR ships pip packages only) and no upstream authentication, so Caddy holds a generated credential and paddlex --serve is pinned to 127.0.0.1 rather than its 0.0.0.0 default. FIRST template with NO VOLUME: stateless, and the pipeline's 5 models (177 MB) are baked into the image because PaddleX downloads them when the SERVER starts, not lazily. The first inference then costs 98s versus ~4s warm, so the entrypoint runs a throwaway inference before the proxy opens. PADDLE_PDX_ENABLE_MKLDNN_BYDEFAULT=False is REQUIRED: oneDNN breaks every request on real x86 with PaddlePaddle 3.3.1, and a local emulated rehearsal cannot reproduce it. Apache-2.0. See guide §5.33.
